# NPCs by location — rendered from the ROM
Generated by `render_npc_by_location.py` from `rop-original.gen` (MD5 `30c18fdbe2183f64702ae9f1a45d63f9`).
Method: `world_enter_location` ($03090A) placement matching, implemented exactly as coded. A template spawns on entry to anchor A (=$FF0E92 & 0x3FF) iff its placement key (template word +20, low 10 bits) equals A (branch 1) or equals the byte at $D1CFC+10*A (branch 2, literal code behavior), AND its key bits 12–15 equal the $FF0E92 variant/floor nibble. Then the kind-0 event-bit gate / seen-ring gate and the +14 time gate apply (see REPORT.md). Names resolve spawn record → name_idx → name table (verified).
Anchor A below is the $D1D00 record index. `dest` is the record's +4 byte ($FF0E98, the room/song-table index); `chunk` = 77+dest; the song record gives the party's entry position. "loc 156" in cutscene docs = dest 156 = anchor 30, NOT anchor 156.

(Anchors 0–29 are (999,999) placeholders and are unreachable as $FF0E92 in normal play.)

## world_tick random-spawn pools (NOT entry spawns)

Anchors 2 and 4 are (999,999) placeholders, so these templates never spawn via `world_enter_location`. `world_tick` ($021BC4) randomly spawns one template with placement key low10 == 2 per tick-window, and ($021D80) one with low10 == 4 (with extra conditions: x-byte vs cell, +14 byte vs threshold, sys_condition_test on the y-byte).

## Appendix: kind-3 events decoded (anchor/nibble for the world_enter_location mode-5 path)

In `npc_attitude_event_apply` the kind-3 path indexes $CF1C0 by +2×24 — all 16 records are out of ROM there (dead). In `world_enter_location` the SAME records are decoded as anchor=(+2 & 0x3FF), nibble=(+2>>12): on entry to that (anchor, nibble) with the event bit set, a mode-5 entity with the record's code spawns.
- ev9: +2=12318 anchor 30 nibble 3 code 768 (cat 12) offset (4,3)
- ev10: +2=12318 anchor 30 nibble 3 code 66 (cat 1) offset (11,4)
- ev23: +2=12387 anchor 99 nibble 3 code 900 (cat 14) offset (6,5)
- ev24: +2=12392 anchor 104 nibble 3 code 1152 (cat 18) offset (3,7)
- ev30: +2=12413 anchor 125 nibble 3 code 1152 (cat 18) offset (9,8)
- ev68: +2=8459 anchor 267 nibble 2 code 775 (cat 12) offset (20,21)
- ev78: +2=12645 anchor 357 nibble 3 code 646 (cat 10) offset (3,5)
- ev85: +2=20871 anchor 391 nibble 5 code 339 (cat 5) offset (3,6)
- ev93: +2=12681 anchor 393 nibble 3 code 648 (cat 10) offset (0,15)
- ev100: +2=8597 anchor 405 nibble 2 code 650 (cat 10) offset (2,5)
- ev108: +2=4499 anchor 403 nibble 1 code 641 (cat 10) offset (8,2)
- ev114: +2=12696 anchor 408 nibble 3 code 863 (cat 13) offset (2,0)
- ev115: +2=12737 anchor 449 nibble 3 code 864 (cat 13) offset (2,1)
- ev126: +2=12609 anchor 321 nibble 3 code 1103 (cat 17) offset (7,9)
- ev137: +2=4440 anchor 344 nibble 1 code 1114 (cat 17) offset (8,6)

## Event-bit lifecycle ($FFC326) — how the gates move

Initial state: `shop_merchant_scan` ($02B5F4, called at $01B75A new-game and $02F904 reset) sets bit *i* = top bit of event *i*'s w0 (i < 161) and clears bits 161–183. Save/load restores the bitmap from the save buffer ($030060 loop, 184 bits). During play the bits move only via: dialog events (first dispatch on rec+10: keys 12 SET / 13 CLEAR / 5 TOGGLE; second dispatch on rec+8: keys 12 SET / 13 CLEAR), script op-11 (`sys_event_run` $02DB06), and shop actions (bit 143 SET at $02DE46; bits 62/63 CLEAR at $02DFDC; bit 180 SET at $02E0C4). Whole-ROM op-11 scan finds 12 real sites ($1A1D8–$1A208 intro, $1A418/$1A420/$1A438/$1A480 Tutu, $1A508 Fezzik); full $D3102 dispatch enumeration finds NO dialog event that sets bits 0–8, 79, or 80.

Initially-CLEAR gating events and their setters:
- bit 6: Buc intro script op-11 $1A1E8 (b3=1) — gates tmpl 4 Roakus @ anchor 47 nib 3
- bit 32: dialog ev231 (2nd dispatch key 12) — gates tmpl 148 Xylotyl @ anchor 123 nib 6
- bit 33: dialog ev226 (1st dispatch key 12) — gates tmpl 138 Nobody @ anchor 125 nib 3
- bit 73: dialog ev372 (2nd dispatch key 12) — gates tmpl 394 Man @ anchor 336 nib 4
- bit 80: Tutu script op-11 $1A420 (b3=1) — gates tmpl 390 Tutu @ anchor 391 nib 5
- bit 100: Fezzik script op-11 $1A508 (b3=1) [mode-5 gate, not a template gate]
- bit 131: dialog ev568 (2nd dispatch key 12) — gates tmpl 333 MR. Belmont @ anchor 321 nib 3
- bit 132: dialog ev149 (1st dispatch key 12) — gates tmpl 334 Lenny @ anchor 321 nib 3
- bit 139: dialog ev465 (1st dispatch key 12) — gates tmpl 193 Yoppa @ anchor 195 nib 3

The three scene swaps this explains:
- **Buc classroom**: bits 0–5 SET at scan, so Thalmus/Roakus-3/Imino/Yoppa/Fezzik-7/Darius-8 spawn on first entry to anchors 30/43/34. The intro script ($1A1B0+, op-11s $1A1D8–$1A208) then CLEARs bits 0–5 and SETs bit 6 — after the scene the classroom cast no longer respawns via placement, and Roakus-4 appears at anchor 47.
- **Tutu tower**: bit 79 SET at scan, so Tutu-389 is standing at anchor 391 nibble 6 (5,3) on first entry — the first meeting works. The Tutu script CLEARs bit 79 ($1A418) and SETs bit 80 ($1A420): on re-entry Tutu-389 is gone and Tutu-390 spawns at nibble 5 (5,4). Bit 81 is cleared then set by the same script ($1A438/$1A480) for its kind-1 tile writes.
- **Fezzik chamber**: bit 99 SET at scan, so Fezzik-342 is standing at anchor 405 nibble 2 (3,8) from the start. The Fezzik script SETs bit 100 ($1A508): on re-entry to 405/nib 2 the kind-3 mode-5 entity (code 650, Ring of Perfctin) spawns.
